- 作者: da Silva JCL, Whelan G, Martins F, Costa TJ, Neves KB
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 04
- DOI: 10.1016/j.vph.2026.107718
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42982211/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

Vascular endothelial growth factor inhibitors (VEGFi) are widely used in the treatment of advanced malignancies, including clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC), but are frequently associated with cardiovascular toxicities such as hypertension and endothelial dysfunction. The molecular mechanisms underlying these adverse effects remain incompletely understood. In this study, we performed an integrated transcriptomic and functional analysis to characterise vascular stress responses associated with VEGFi. Pre-treatment tumour RNA sequencing data from a public cancer cohort were analysed using Gene Set Enrichment Analysis (GSEA) to identify pathway-level signatures associated with subsequent VEGFi treatment. No individual genes remained significant after multiple testing correction; therefore, emphasis was placed on coordinated biological responses. Functional validation was conducted in human vascular cells treated with a VEGFi to assess reactive oxygen species (ROS) production, cytokine levels and expression, and extracellular vesicles (EVs) release. Pathway analysis revealed significant baseline enrichment of oxidative stress, inflammatory response, and nuclear factor-kappa B (NF-κB) signalling pathways in tumours of patients who subsequently received VEGFi. These baseline associations were complemented by increased vascular cell ROS production and enhanced EVs release in vitro. Inflammatory activation was further confirmed by increased cytokine secretion, despite unchanged messenger RNA expression, indicating regulation at post-transcriptional and secretory levels. Collectively, these baseline tumour signatures and the in vitro vascular findings are consistent with a model in which VEGF inhibition induces a coordinated redox-inflammatory response, which may involve EV-mediated intercellular communication. This mechanism may contribute to vascular dysfunction and cardiovascular toxicity associated with VEGFi-targeted therapies.

#### [A] An endothelial-specific TIE2-L914F mouse model reveals cellular mechanisms of venous malformation progression and recapitulates the patients' response to targeted therapy.

- PMID: 42990562
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: TEK/TIE2 appears with vascular-malformation context
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology
- 作者: Bischoff LJ, Schrenk S, Barney A, Soroko K, Sherpa C, Simons M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 30
- DOI: 10.64898/2026.10.29.762550
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42990562/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-07

#### 摘要

TIE2 p.L914F is the most common driver mutation of sporadic venous malformation (VM), a disease characterized by massively enlarged veins. The study of the molecular and cellular mechanisms contributing to TIE2-driven VM has been hindered by the lack of a genetic in vivo model. We generated a mouse system in which TIE2 L914F is expressed postnatally in endothelial cells (EC). Mutant mice develop visible lesions with dilated veins with irregular smooth muscle cell coverage, histologically mimicking patients' VM. With the use of an mCherry reporter we demonstrate that lesions form within the venous vasculature, and mutant ECs undergo clonal expansion fueled by a biphasic mechanism consisting of an early burst of proliferation followed by cell size increase. Targeted treatment with rapamycin partially reduces lesion growth by targeting increased proliferation and prolongs mouse survival, demonstrating this genetic murine model recapitulates the patients' therapeutic response. Together, these findings provide new cellular and mechanistic insights into TIE2 p.L914F-driven venous malformation and establish a genetic platform for preclinical therapeutic studies.

### glycolysis / mitochondria / metabolic reprogramming（1）

#### [A] Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor-Induced Cardiovascular Disease: The Role of Accelerated Senescence in Vascular Dysfunction.

- PMID: 42850906
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Antioxidants & redox signaling
- 作者: Casso-Chapa B, Paniagua-Bojorges A, Palaskas NL, Nead KT, Samanthapudi VSK, Lee J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1177/15230864251410546
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42850906/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-07

#### 摘要

SIGNIFICANCE: Immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) have transformed cancer therapy by reactivating antitumor immunity and improving outcomes across multiple malignancies. Yet, clinical observations and preclinical studies suggest that ICIs may increase long-term cardiovascular disease risk, including hypertension, atherosclerosis, and thrombosis. The underlying mechanisms remain incompletely defined, highlighting the need for focused investigation into the vascular consequences of immune activation.
RECENT ADVANCES: ICI-induced immune activation disrupts vascular and immune homeostasis through persistent production of reactive oxygen species (ROS), mitochondrial dysfunction, and accumulation of cell-free DNA (cfDNA). Sustained T-cell activation enhances ROS generation, driving DNA damage and senescence in both endothelial and immune cells. ICIs may also promote endothelial-to-mesenchymal transition (EndoMT) through chronic oxidative and inflammatory signaling, contributing to fibrosis, vascular stiffness, and chronic inflammation. Transforming growth factor-beta, although immunosuppressive in many contexts, can drive profibrotic and EndoMT programs within the vasculature. Other cytokines and regulators, including interleukin-17 and regulator of calcineurin 1, have been implicated in vascular remodeling, atherosclerosis, and hypertension.
CRITICAL ISSUES: The relative roles of ROS, mitochondrial dysfunction, cfDNA-driven inflammation, and EndoMT in ICI-associated vascular senescence remain unclear. While inflammation-induced vascular damage is well-recognized, direct ICI-specific mechanisms require stronger evidence. How senescent endothelial and immune cells influence therapy resistance and remodeling of the tumor microenvironment also remains to be defined.
FUTURE DIRECTIONS: Mitochondrial and cytosolic ROS sources-arising from electron leakage during oxidative phosphorylation and NADPH oxidases activity-shape immune signaling and sustain T-cell activation during checkpoint blockade. The DNA damage response kinases ataxia-telangiectasia mutated and ATM- and Rad3-related, activated by oxidative and replicative stress, engage p53-dependent senescence pathways in endothelial and immune cells. Future studies should evaluate biomarkers such as cfDNA and thrombospondin-1 and explore senescence-targeting or redox-modulating interventions that may alleviate cardiovascular toxicity without diminishing antitumor efficacy. This review outlines mechanistic pathways linking ICIs to vascular dysfunction and senescence, integrating preclinical data with emerging clinical insights. It distinguishes established processes from ICI-specific hypotheses and identifies key gaps requiring clinical validation. Antioxid. Redox Signal. 45, 821-839.

## 4. 血浆置换 / 换血（25）

### [A] The double-edged sword: a contemporary perspective on biologics for anti-cytokine syndromes.

- PMID: 42965416
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Expert opinion on biological therapy
- 作者: Chiang V, Man MPH, Li PH
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 31
- DOI: 10.1080/14712598.2026.2758529
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42965416/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-02

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Anti-cytokine autoantibodies (ACA) modulate human immunity and may manifest as phenocopies of inborn errors of immunity. Patients typically present with severe, persistent, unusual, or recurrent infections and possible autoimmune manifestations. While prolonged antimicrobials are the traditional standard of care, they frequently lead to drug resistance and adverse effects, highlighting an urgent need for disease-modifying therapies. However, the use of immunosuppressive agents in treating immunodeficiency is a dilemma in the field.
AREAS COVERED: This review synthesizes current clinical evidence on managing ACA syndromes, with specific focus on interferon-γ, granulocyte-macrophage colony stimulating factor, IL-12/23, IL-6, type 1 IFNs, and IL-10. We evaluate the efficacy and therapeutic rationale of various treatments, including B-cell depletion, plasma exchange, and cytokine replacement, while highlighting the distinct clinical phenotypes and diagnostic strategies for each syndrome.
EXPERT OPINION: The use of biologics in ACA marks a shift from reactive antimicrobial treatments to proactive targeted immune reconstitution. Clinical use is still restricted by the inherent risks of immunosuppressing actively infected patients and the absence of standardized functional assays for monitoring. Overcoming these barriers will require accessible ACA screening and the development of customized therapies, such as chimeric autoantibody receptor-T, to deliver functional cures and improve long-term patient care.

### [A] Hyponatremia in septic shock: Pathophysiology, fluid resuscitation challenges, and neurological complications.

- PMID: 42965188
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Journal of family medicine and primary care
- 作者: Alkhaldi MR
- 发表日期: 2026 Jul
- DOI: 10.4103/jfmpc.jfmpc_369_26
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42965188/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-02

#### 摘要

Sepsis is a life-threatening condition characterized by organ dysfunction due to a dysregulated host response to infection, with septic shock representing its most severe form and carrying high mortality in intensive care units (ICUs). Among critically ill patients, hyponatremia (serum sodium) <135 mmol/L is a common electrolyte disturbance associated with worse outcomes. While prior studies have explored hypernatremia in septic shock, the pathophysiology, clinical implications, and management of hyponatremia in this context remain underexplored, creating uncertainty for clinicians regarding fluid resuscitation strategies and neurological safety. This narrative review aimed to synthesize current evidence on hyponatremia in septic shock, focusing on underlying mechanisms, resuscitation challenges, and neurological complications. A comprehensive literature search was conducted in PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, EMBASE, and Google Scholar for studies published between 2017 and 2025, including clinical cohorts, case reports, and systematic reviews. The findings indicate that hyponatremia in septic shock arises from non-osmotic vasopressin release, systemic inflammation, acute kidney injury, and fluid shifts during aggressive resuscitation. Large-volume crystalloid administration may exacerbate sodium derangements, while rapid correction poses a risk of osmotic demyelination syndrome (ODS), even under guideline-adherent practices. Case reports suggest plasma exchange may provide the most consistent benefit in severe neurological complications. In conclusion, hyponatremia in septic shock reflects complex neuroendocrine, inflammatory, and renal interactions. Optimized management requires individualized fluid strategies, cautious sodium correction, and prolonged neurological monitoring to mitigate complications and improve outcomes in critically ill patients.

#### 摘要

This case report documents a rare case of scrub typhus with multiple serious complications in a 8-year-old patient. Scrub typhus is usually more prevalent in adults, but serious complications in children are uncommon. This report examines a severe pediatric case involving septic shock, acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS), and hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis (HLH). The patient initially presented with erythema of the umbilicus, which then progressed to characteristic crusting with high fever, hepatosplenomegaly, and enlarged lymph nodes. Metagenomic next-generation sequencing (mNGS) confirmed the presence of Scrub typhus in the patient's blood sample. Notably, this is the first case of scrub typhus found in lung using mNGS, providing strong evidence for early detection. Treatment included a combination of antibiotics, particularly doxycycline and rifampicin, as well as supportive measures such as invasive mechanical ventilation, plasma exchange, continuous renal replacement therapy (CRRT) and chemotherapy. With this comprehensive treatment approach, the patient's condition gradually improved and he was eventually discharged with complete recovery. This case emphasizes the importance of timely and accurate diagnosis and multidisciplinary supportive care in the treatment of severe scrub typhus in children.

### [A] Acute kidney injury associated with acute fatty liver of pregnancy: An update on a rare clinical entity.

- PMID: 41479817
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: World journal of nephrology
- 作者: Rajput M, Bachani S, Suri J, Bharti R, Kumar A, Kumar R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 25
- DOI: 10.5527/wjn.v14.i4.110414
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41479817/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-02

#### 摘要

Acute fatty liver of pregnancy (AFLP) is a rare but potentially life-threatening liver disease associated with mitochondrial dysfunction. It is characterized by microvesicular hepatic steatosis and typically occurs in the third trimester, though it may rarely present postpartum. AFLP is considered a non-thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) but may present with overlapping TMA features. Its incidence ranges from 1 in 7000 to 1 in 20000 pregnancies, although milder cases may go unrecognized. AFLP can rapidly progress to acute liver failure and 20% to 40% of affected women exhibit clinical features of preeclampsia. Acute kidney injury (AKI) is a frequent complication, observed in 55% to 75% of AFLP cases, which is significantly higher than the 7% to 20% occurrence seen in preeclampsia or hemolysis, elevated liver enzymes, and low platelets syndrome. The exact mechanism behind AKI in AFLP remains unclear, but renal histology has shown tubular deposits of free fatty acids, which correlate with current theories regarding liver pathology. While AFLP-associated AKI is often reversible after delivery, some patients may develop persistent AKI that requires dialysis. Therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE) has been explored in these cases, but available evidence is limited. This review summarizes the current understanding of the epidemiology, pathophysiology, clinical features, and management of AKI in the context of AFLP, and discusses the potential role of adjunctive therapies such as TPE.

### [A] Baseline severity and early tacrolimus trough are associated with time to minimal manifestations in steroid-free tacrolimus monotherapy for AChR antibody-positive myasthenia gravis.

- PMID: 42971878
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Revue neurologique
- 作者: Uchi T, Konno S
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 02
- DOI: 10.1016/j.neurol.2026.10.008
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42971878/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Tacrolimus is used as an immunosuppressive treatment for myasthenia gravis (MG), and monotherapy without corticosteroids may be feasible in selected patients. Still, what determines how quickly patients reach minimal manifestations (MM) under this approach has not been well characterized.
OBJECTIVE: To examine the time course to MM achievement and to investigate whether baseline disease severity and early tacrolimus trough concentration are associated with the time to MM achievement in acetylcholine receptor (AChR) antibody-positive MG.
METHODS: This single-center retrospective cohort study included 30 AChR antibody-positive MG patients who started tacrolimus monotherapy (3mg/day) within 1-3months after onset, without oral corticosteroids or early additional non-oral immunomodulatory treatments. These additional treatments included intravenous methylprednisolone pulse therapy, intravenous immunoglobulin, plasmapheresis, and molecular targeted therapies. The primary outcome was the time from tacrolimus initiation to first MM achievement. Clinical courses were compared between ocular MG (OMG) and generalized MG (GMG) using the Kaplan-Meier method. A Cox proportional hazards model stratified by baseline phenotype was used for the main analysis. Exploratory quartile-based Kaplan-Meier curves were also generated according to early tacrolimus trough concentration.
RESULTS: Overall, 26 of 30 patients (86.7%) achieved MM, and the median time to MM achievement was 8.0months. The time to MM was shorter in OMG than in GMG (log-rank P=0.044). In the phenotype-stratified Cox model, higher early tacrolimus trough concentration was associated with faster MM achievement (hazard ratio [HR] 1.38, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.15-1.66; P<0.001), whereas higher baseline Myasthenia Gravis Composite (MGC) score was associated with slower MM achievement (HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.70-0.92; P=0.002). Exploratory quartile-based curves showed a descriptive tendency toward earlier MM achievement in patients with higher early trough concentrations.
CONCLUSIONS: Early tacrolimus trough concentration and baseline MGC were linked to the time to MM achievement under steroid-free tacrolimus monotherapy in AChR antibody-positive MG. These variables warrant prospective evaluation as early indicators for treatment reassessment.

### [A] An Approach to Acute Cranial Symptoms in Primary Care: Lesson From a Case of Miller Fisher Syndrome.

- PMID: 42970499
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 期刊: The American journal of case reports
- 作者: Jasme H, Mohd Noh MN, Zainal-Abidin ANI
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 02
- DOI: 10.12659/AJCR.954763
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42970499/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND Miller Fisher syndrome (MFS) is a rare variant of Guillain-Barre syndrome characterized by the classic triad of ophthalmoplegia, ataxia, and areflexia following a recent infection. Early recognition is particularly challenging due to the rarity of the condition and its tendency to mimic other common neurological and non-neurological disorders. We report a case highlighting the diagnostic and referral challenges encountered in the primary care setting. CASE REPORT A 38-year-old woman with no significant comorbidity presented to a primary care clinic with acute diplopia, right eye pain, headache, and perioral numbness following an upper respiratory tract infection 2 weeks earlier. Initial neurological examination was unremarkable. She was referred to the ophthalmology and emergency departments to exclude ocular pathology and posterior circulation stroke. Laboratory investigation and computed tomography of the brain were unremarkable and she was managed as a case of migraine. Meanwhile, ophthalmological review subsequently demonstrated ophthalmoplegia and the following day she developed bulbar symptoms prompting urgent neurological consultation. Repeated neurological examination revealed the classic triad of MFS. She received plasma exchange therapy following her clinical deterioration. Her neurological symptoms gradually improved with complete recovery, with residual diplopia resolving 8 weeks after symptom onset. CONCLUSIONS MFS remains a predominantly clinical diagnosis with subtle and evolving features that may delay recognition in the primary care setting. This case underscores the crucial role of primary care physicians in maintaining a high index of suspicion, recognizing atypical presentation, and providing appropriate safety-netting and timely specialist referral to facilitate early diagnosis and improve clinical outcomes.

### [A] Age: a modifiable risk factor for cardiovascular disease?

- PMID: 42970081
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Herz
- 作者: Kafka A, Becker L, Hauner H, Wackerhage H
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 02
- DOI: 10.1007/s00059-026-05398-2
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42970081/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

Age is the strongest risk factor for cardiovascular disease, yet biological aging is increasingly recognized as a modifiable process. Animal studies over the past few decades have demonstrated that genetic, pharmacological, and dietary interventions can extend the healthspan and lifespan of animals by targeting conserved mechanisms of aging. Examining whether lifespan-extending interventions work in humans is challenging due to the long lifespan of our species. However, the development of biomarkers of biological aging or "aging clocks" has allowed for human trials where the rate of biological aging can be estimated. These surrogate markers now enable the first generation of human geroscience trials, in which interventions such as caloric restriction, rapamycin, taurine, plasma exchange, or nutritional strategies are evaluated for their ability to slow biological aging. Particularly relevant for cardiology are recently developed organ- and cell-specific aging clocks that estimate the biological age of the heart, vasculature, and cardiovascular cell types. These biomarkers may be suitable for improving cardiovascular risk prediction and providing surrogate outcomes for trials targeting cardiovascular aging. Together, these advances suggest that slowing biological aging could complement established cardiovascular prevention and treatment strategies in the future.

### [A] Atraumatic iliopsoas heterotopic ossification with postoperative atypical haemolytic uraemic syndrome.

- PMID: 42951321

- 期刊: BMJ case reports
- 作者: Dhankhar K, Shrivastava S, Kumar N, Shetty T
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.1136/bcr-2026-272969
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42951321/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

We describe a case of a middle-aged woman presenting with a 2-year history of progressive, painless restriction of left hip movement without preceding trauma or surgery. Imaging demonstrated a well-defined ossified mass within the iliopsoas muscle exhibiting a classical zonal maturation pattern. Following unsuccessful conservative therapy, delayed surgical excision after radiological maturation was undertaken and histopathology confirmed heterotopic ossification. On postoperative day 3, she developed acute anaemia, thrombocytopenia and severe renal impairment. Laboratory evaluation supported thrombotic microangiopathy, with atypical haemolytic uraemic syndrome considered the most probable diagnosis. The patient was managed with therapeutic plasma exchange and temporary haemodialysis, resulting in full haematological and renal recovery. At follow-up, hip function improved substantially with no evidence of recurrence. This case emphasises the need to recognise uncommon atraumatic iliopsoas heterotopic ossification and to maintain vigilance for postoperative thrombotic microangiopathy to ensure early multidisciplinary intervention and favourable outcomes.

### [A] Case Report: Atypical hemolytic uremic syndrome triggered by Rhinovirus/Enterovirus infection with cutaneous thrombotic microangiopathy requiring surgical management.

- PMID: 42973488
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in pediatrics
- 作者: Shen J, Cai K, Kang W, He Q, Zhang J, Yin L
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fped.2026.1973640
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42973488/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

Atypical hemolytic uremic syndrome (aHUS) represents a complement-mediated thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) characterized by microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, thrombocytopenia, and end-organ damage, most frequently affecting the kidneys. Although infection is a well-recognized precipitant, extensive skin involvement is uncommon. Here, we describe a 14-year-old boy who developed aHUS following Rhinovirus/Enterovirus infection. He was found to have anemia, thrombocytopenia, acute kidney injury, hematuria, and proteinuria, and peripheral smear examination demonstrated schistocytes. Complement evaluation showed reduced C3 and factor I concentrations, increased soluble C5b-9, and preserved ADAMTS13 activity. Investigations for Shiga toxin-producing Escherichia coli, systemic autoimmune disease, and alternative infectious etiologies were negative. Even with eculizumab, corticosteroids, therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE), and continuous renal replacement therapy (CRRT), his condition progressed to petechiae, ecchymoses, hemorrhagic bullae, and necrotic lesions of the axilla, neck, and back. Since the necrotic areas failed to heal and were accompanied by continued platelet consumption and a heightened risk of infection, surgical debridement and wound closure were undertaken after the platelet count reached 81 × 10^9/L. Histopathologic analysis of the excised tissue confirmed cutaneous thrombotic microangiopathy with microvascular thrombosis. Following the procedure, platelet count, hemoglobin, and complement C3 returned to normal, and the wound healed without complication. At subsequent follow-up, the scars were supple and level, with no sensory deficit or functional limitation. This report expands the range of infectious triggers associated with aHUS and demonstrates that persistent necrotic cutaneous thrombotic microangiopathy may necessitate prompt surgical management in addition to complement inhibition and supportive treatment.

### [A] Clinical improvement despite rising anti-streptococcal antibody titers during adjunctive bacteriophage therapy in refractory PANDAS: a hypothesis-generating case report.

- PMID: 42973455
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in medicine
- 作者: Recordare A, Dadiani M, Nizharadze D, Nadareishvili L, Pirozzolo G, Palumbo R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fmed.2026.1973954
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42973455/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Pediatric Autoimmune Neuropsychiatric Disorders Associated with Streptococcal infections (PANDAS) are characterized by the abrupt onset of anxiety, obsessive-compulsive symptoms, tics, and other neuropsychiatric manifestations associated with Group A Streptococcus exposure. Although antimicrobial and immunomodulatory therapies are widely employed, many patients experience persistent or relapsing symptoms despite prolonged treatment. Bacteriophage therapy has not previously been reported in PANDAS and represents an experimental, pathogen-directed therapeutic approach.
CASE PRESENTATION: We report the case of an 11-year-old girl with severe refractory PANDAS characterized by obsessive-compulsive symptoms, anxiety, motor and vocal tics, emotional dysregulation, insomnia, agitation, and marked functional impairment, including school refusal. The diagnosis was supported by elevated antistreptococcal serology. The patient received several escalating interventions, including intramuscular benzathine penicillin, azithromycin, plasma exchange, intravenous immunoglobulins, psychotherapy, and aripiprazole. A clinical improvement was initially observed after penicillin administration, although the benefit was incomplete and transient. No active streptococcal isolate was available for phage susceptibility testing. On the basis of repeated antibiotic responsiveness, empirical anti-streptococcal bacteriophage therapy was initiated using Pyo Bacteriophages (Eliava biopreparation), administered twice daily for three 15-day cycles as add-on therapy, while antibiotics were maintained.
RESULTS: Clinical sustained improvement became evident approximately six days after initiation of bacteriophage therapy, mirroring the latency previously observed after penicillin, and was followed by sustained clinical stabilization. No adverse events were reported. Serial serological evaluation demonstrated a paradoxical increase in ASO and anti-DNase titres despite continuous neurological and psychiatric improvement.
CONCLUSIONS: This case suggests that bacteriophage therapy may represent a plausible pathogen-directed strategy in selected patients with refractory PANDAS, particularly when a reproducible clinical response to anti-streptococcal antibiotics suggests persistent microbial involvement. More importantly, the observed dissociation between rising anti-streptococcal antibody titers and sustained clinical improvement raises the possibility that serological kinetics may not accurately reflect disease activity during effective pathogen-directed therapy. One possible explanation is that bacteriophage-induced bacterial lysis increased antigen exposure while simultaneously reducing the bacterial burden responsible for persistent immune activation. However, because bacteriophages were administered as add-on therapy in a single uncontrolled case, causality cannot be established. Prospective studies are required to determine the role of bacteriophage-based interventions in post-infectious neuroimmune disorders.

### [A] A national survey of autoimmune glomerular disease services in the UK.

- PMID: 42972294
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: BMC nephrology
- 作者: Francis L, Barratt J, Hollis J, Willcocks L, Jones RB
- 发表日期: 2026 Aug 05
- DOI: 10.1186/s12882-026-05257-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42972294/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Glomerulonephritis (GN) accounts for 20%-25% of chronic kidney disease and is a major cause of end-stage kidney disease. The 2021 UK Rare Disease Framework highlighted inequities in rare disease care, with commitments to integrated care and equitable access. This national survey explored unmet needs in GN services, particularly service delivery, access to novel therapies, and research opportunities.
METHODS: An online survey was designed by the UK Kidney Research Consortium Glomerulonephritis Clinical Study Group and distributed to UK adult and paediatric nephrologists. The 25-item survey assessed service infrastructure and staffing, equity of access to novel therapies, and research infrastructure and barriers. Responses were analysed using descriptive statistics and qualitative content analysis.
RESULTS: Eighty-five responses were received from all eight NHS England renal networks and from Northern Ireland, Scotland, and Wales, including 73 adult respondents from 57 renal centres and 12 paediatric nephrologists from 10 paediatric centres. After removing duplicate responses per centre, 67% (38/57) of adult respondents from 57 centres reported working in a tertiary renal centre. Overall, 67% (38/57) reported a dedicated primary glomerulonephritis, vasculitis or lupus clinic, 49% (28/57) cohorted patients by disease subtype, and 32% (18/57) ran more than one clinic per week. Most reported access to day-case infusion services 93% (53/57), plasma exchange 77% (44/57), and in-house multidisciplinary teams 74% (42/57). There was a reported absence of a specialist clinic in 33% (19/57) of centres, in-house glomerular disease specialist consultant support 30% (17/57), specialist nurses 61% (35/57), and renal research nurses 18% (10/57). Regional multidisciplinary meetings and dedicated patient messaging systems were unavailable for 58% (33/57) and 60% (34/57) respectively. In England, access to rituximab, avacopan, belimumab and voclosporin was variable. Research activity was reported by 72% (41/57) for commercial and 56% (32/57) for non-commercial studies. Most respondents 96% (55/57) expressed a desire to increase research involvement, and 81% (46/57) wished to lead their own studies. Reported barriers in embedding research into clinical practice included lack of clinician time, insufficient job-plan allocation, limited research nurse availability and funding. Paediatric respondents also reported similar constraints.
CONCLUSIONS: This survey highlights disparity in access to specialist clinics, specialist nurse support and regional MDTs in glomerular disease across the UK. Nephrologists remain highly motivated to conduct research, though capacity and funding constraints persist.
CLINICAL TRIAL NUMBER: Not applicable.

### [A] A rare neurological adverse event of palbociclib: a chronic inflammatory demyelinating polyneuropathy (CIDP) mimic.

- PMID: 42980868
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 作者: Zeynoun J, Evans M, Boldig K, Ali H
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 04
- DOI: 10.1007/s10549-026-08081-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42980868/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-05

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: Hormone receptor-positive (HR+)/human epidermal growth factor receptor 2-negative (HER2-) breast cancer accounts for approximately 65-70% of all breast cancer cases. First-line treatment for metastatic HR+/HER2- disease typically includes endocrine therapy combined with a CDK4/6 inhibitor, achieving progression-free survival (PFS) of 24-33 months with preserved quality of life. Although CDK4/6 inhibitors are generally well tolerated, they are associated with hematologic and gastrointestinal toxicities. We describe a patient who developed features of chronic immune-mediate polyradiculoneuropathy associated with neurofascin antibodies following initiation of palbociclib.
METHODS: We conducted a detailed clinical review of a patient with metastatic HR+/HER2- breast cancer who developed progressive bilateral upper and lower extremity weakness after starting palbociclib. Clinical presentation, laboratory findings, imaging studies, electrodiagnostic testing, and treatment response were reviewed.
RESULTS: A 43-year old female with metastatic HR+/HER2- breast cancer developed asymmetric progressive weakness and sensory symptoms approximately two weeks after initiating palbociclib. Electrodiagnostic testing demonstrated a mixed axonal and demyelinating sensorimotor polyneuropathy meeting EAN/PNS criteria for an acquired inflammatory demyelinating polyradiculoneuropathy. Initial diagnostic considerations included acute inflammatory demyelinating polyradiculoneuropathy (AIDP) and multifocal CIDP (formerly termed multifocal acquired demyelinating sensory and motor neuropathy [MADSAM]). Despite initial improvement following treatment with IVIG, the patient experienced continued neurologic progression requiring plasmapheresis, repeat IVIG, high-dose corticosteroids, and rituximab. Subsequent testing demonstrated anti-neurofascin-155 and anti-neurofascin-186 antibody positivity. Longitudinal follow-up demonstrated significant functional recovery following rituximab therapy.
CONCLUSION: This case suggests a possible association between palbociclib exposure and the development of chronic immune-mediate polyradiculoneuropathy associated with neurofascin antibodies. Clinicians should consider immune mediated neuropathies in patients receiving CDK4/6 inhibitor therapy who develop weakness, sensory deficits, and areflexia. Further investigation is warranted to better characterize potential immunologic effects of CDK4/6 inhibition and their relationship to autoimmune neurologic disease.

### [A] Anti-N-Methyl-D-Aspartate Receptor Encephalitis Presenting as Acute Psychosis in a Renal Transplant Recipient Under Chronic Immunosuppression.

- PMID: 42979341

- DOI: 10.64719/pb.21633

#### 摘要

Anti-N-methyl-D-aspartate receptor (anti-NMDAR) encephalitis is the most frequent autoimmune encephalitis and characteristically debuts with psychiatric symptoms, which delays etiologic diagnosis. Its occurrence in patients immunosuppressed by post-transplant therapy is exceptional, with fewer than ten reported cases, most of them with unfavorable outcomes. We describe a 38-year-old woman with a 13-year-old renal graft and stage 4 chronic kidney disease, maintained on tacrolimus and mycophenolate, who was admitted to an acute psychiatric unit with abrupt-onset psychosis comprising mystical-megalomanic delusions, auditory hallucinations, and severe psychomotor agitation. Three weeks later, productive symptoms ceased abruptly and a rigid-akinetic syndrome emerged, accompanied by fever, autonomic instability, and bilateral mydriasis. Because rigidity and mutism followed an increase in aripiprazole dosage, neuroleptic malignant syndrome was initially suspected; mydriasis, characteristically absent in that condition, together with persistence of the neurological syndrome after antipsychotic withdrawal, redirected the diagnosis. Cerebrospinal fluid showed mononuclear pleocytosis and elevated protein, and anti-GluN1 antibodies were positive in serum and cerebrospinal fluid. Sequential immunotherapy with intravenous methylprednisolone, immunoglobulin, and plasmapheresis was administered, and exploratory oophorectomy revealed no teratoma. The patient achieved near-complete functional recovery, with only mild residual attentional impairment at 14 months. This case illustrates the diagnostic overlap between autoimmune encephalitis and primary psychotic disorders at first psychiatric contact, identifies pupillary dilation as a clinically useful discriminating sign against neuroleptic malignant syndrome, and documents a favorable outcome in a setting where previously published post-transplant cases have fared poorly.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND/OBJECTIVES: Systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE)-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) is a severe complication with high mortality. This study aimed to examine clinical manifestations, treatment strategies, and outcomes in these patients.
METHODS: This retrospective cohort study included 64 patients diagnosed with SLE-associated TMA between January 2016 and April 2025. Patients were stratified by ADAMTS13 activity into SLE-associated immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (SLE-iTTP, ADAMTS13 <10%) and non-iTTP TMA (ADAMTS13 ≥10%) groups. Clinical characteristics, laboratory findings, treatment responses, and outcomes were compared between the groups. Within the SLE-iTTP subgroup, outcomes were compared between patients receiving rituximab (RTX) and those receiving cyclophosphamide (CYC) as adjunctive immunosuppressive therapy.
RESULTS: Among 64 patients, 36 (56.3%) were classified as SLE-iTTP and 28 (43.7%) as non-iTTP TMA. Patients with SLE-iTTP exhibited more severe thrombocytopenia (median platelet count: 5.5 vs 39 × 109/L, p<0.001), lower hemoglobin levels (49.5 vs 69 g/L, p<0.001), and a higher frequency of neuropsychiatric manifestations (88.9% vs 57.1%, p=0.008). Anti-dsDNA positivity (86.1% vs 57.1%, p=0.012) and hypocomplementemia (94.4% vs 71.4%, p=0.016) were more prevalent in the SLE-iTTP group. Within the SLE-iTTP subgroup, all patients received glucocorticoids and plasma exchange. Patients treated with RTX (n=20) achieved a significantly higher rate of clinical remission or response than those treated with CYC (n=16) (85.0% vs 50.0%, p=0.034) and showed faster platelet count normalization (median 14 vs 21 days, p=0.018). Overall mortality was 34.4% (22/64).
CONCLUSION: ADAMTS13 activity identifies a distinct subset of SLE-associated TMA with characteristic features and differential treatment responses. In SLE-iTTP, RTX-based regimens was associated with better clinical efficacy than CYC, supporting a pathophysiology-guided therapeutic approach for this life-threatening condition.

### [A] Peri-transplant eculizumab for preventing antibody-mediated rejection in kidney transplant recipients at high immunologic risk: a systematic review with design-stratified evidence synthesis.

- PMID: 42978583
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in immunology
- 作者: Liang C, Bao R, Yin S, Song T, Lin T
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1982870
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42978583/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-05

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Highly sensitized candidates, preformed donor-specific antibodies, and ABO incompatibility restrict access to kidney transplantation and increase antibody-mediated rejection (AMR) risk. We systematically evaluated peri-transplant eculizumab-containing prophylaxis in high-immunologic-risk kidney transplantation.
METHODS: We searched PubMed, Europe PMC, Ovid Embase, Web of Science Core Collection, Ovid CENTRAL, ClinicalTrials.gov, WHO ICTRP, and supplementary sources; database searches ended 10 June 2026, and registry searches ended 20 August 2026.
RESULTS: A total of 31 reports were included; 30 were linked to 14 studies or registry projects, and one pathology report was retained qualitatively. In the only randomized trial, centrally confirmed AMR occurred in 5/51 recipients in both the eculizumab and standard-desensitization groups [risk ratio (RR) 1.00, 95% confidence interval (CI) 0.31-3.25]. A historical comparison reported AMR in 2/26 versus 21/51 recipients (RR 0.19, 95% CI 0.05-0.74), but treatment era, post-transplant plasma exchange, and clinical management changed concurrently. An 80-recipient single-arm study reported three AMR events by week 9. Long-term follow-up showed no consistent graft-survival or kidney-function advantage, while persistent donor-specific antibodies and chronic microvascular injury remained evident.
DISCUSSION: Eculizumab can provide terminal complement blockade within selected combination prophylaxis, but randomized evidence does not establish an incremental reduction in AMR. Definitive trials require standardized background therapy, rescue criteria, long-term graft outcomes, and infection surveillance. This systematic review was registered in PROSPERO (CRD420261484542).
SYSTEMATIC REVIEW REGISTRATION: https://www.crd.york.ac.uk/PROSPERO/, identifier CRD420261484542.

### [A] Immune-Mediated Demyelinating Central Nervous System Graft-Versus-Host Disease After Allogeneic Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation.

- PMID: 42979616
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 期刊: Journal of Brown hospital medicine
- 作者: Ugo M, Shaikh A, Sidani C, Duarte ML, Freitas LF
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.56305/001c.168646
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42979616/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-05

#### 摘要

Central nervous system graft-versus-host disease (CNS GVHD) is a rare and challenging complication of allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT) that can mimic infection, malignancy, or primary demyelinating disorders. We describe a 21-year-old man with high-risk Philadelphia chromosome-like B-cell acute lymphoblastic leukemia underwent matched unrelated donor allogeneic HSCT after achieving measurable residual disease negativity. His post-transplant course was complicated by hepatic and cutaneous GVHD. Sixteen months after transplantation, he developed progressive bilateral lower-extremity weakness, gait ataxia, and blurred vision. Brain MRI demonstrated multifocal subcortical lesions, while spinal MRI revealed enhancing intramedullary lesions at multiple levels. Orbital MRI showed bilateral optic neuritis. Extensive infectious, malignant, and autoimmune evaluations were unrevealing. Given his prior GVHD, multifocal demyelinating pattern, exclusion of alternative etiologies, and response to immunosuppression, probable demyelinating CNS GVHD was diagnosed. He was treated with corticosteroids, IVIG, plasma exchange, and rituximab, followed by maintenance IVIG and prednisone, with substantial clinical and radiographic improvement. CNS GVHD should be considered in HSCT recipients with new multifocal neurologic deficits and demyelinating lesions, particularly with antecedent systemic GVHD. Early recognition and immunosuppressive treatment may improve neurologic outcomes.

### [A] Vaping-associated acute tubular injury presenting as a pulmonary-renal syndrome in an adolescent.

- PMID: 42986418

- 作者: Piskin CC, Atmis B, Ok ZO, Ates KE, Gonlusen G, Bayazit AK
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 05
- DOI: 10.1007/s00467-026-07629-4
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42986418/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

Electronic cigarette use is increasingly common among adolescents and is associated with a widening range of toxic effects, although renal complications remain poorly characterized and are rarely confirmed histologically in this age group. We describe a previously healthy 14-year-old boy, a regular e-cigarette user, who developed oliguria, dark-colored urine, severe hypertension, seizures, and pulmonary infiltrates two days after his last use. Serum creatinine rose to 10.6 mg/dL, requiring hemodialysis. The presentation raised concern for a pulmonary-renal syndrome, prompting empiric corticosteroids and plasma exchange. However, serological testing was negative, urinary β2-microglobulin was markedly elevated, and kidney biopsy showed acute tubular injury without glomerular or immune-mediated disease. Immunosuppression was withdrawn, and kidney function recovered completely, remaining normal at 18 months. This case shows that vaping-associated acute tubular injury can mimic a pulmonary-renal syndrome and highlights the value of kidney biopsy in adolescents with unexplained severe acute kidney injury.

#### 摘要

Neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorder (NMOSD) can cause extensive brain lesions and severe deficits. A 53-year-old man with aquaporin-4 immunoglobulin G (AQP4-IgG) -positive NMOSD developed rapidly progressive hemiparesis, extensive bilateral cerebral lesions, and impaired communication. Despite intravenous methylprednisolone, his condition worsened, with an Expanded Disability Status Scale (EDSS) score of 9.5. Following plasma exchange and intravenous immunoglobulin, with rituximab introduced for relapse prevention, he gradually regained the ability to walk with a unilateral cane (EDSS 6.0). NMOSD and serum AQP4-IgG testing should be considered in patients with extensive tumefactive cerebral lesions, even without optic neuritis or myelitis.

### [A] Probable Deferasirox-Associated Acute-on-Chronic Liver Failure in Non-Transfusion-Dependent β-Thalassemia with Iron-Related Cirrhosis: A Case Report.

#### 摘要

Oral iron chelation with deferasirox is widely used to manage iron overload in patients with thalassemia syndromes. Clinically significant drug-induced liver injury (DILI) attributed to deferasirox is uncommon but has been reported. We describe a 49-year-old man with non-transfusion-dependent β-thalassemia intermedia (β-thalassemia trait with α-globin gene triplication), substantial iron overload, cirrhosis, and previously treated hepatocellular carcinoma who developed acute hepatic decompensation during deferasirox therapy. He presented with progressive jaundice, ascites, and encephalopathy. Laboratory evaluation showed profound conjugated hyperbilirubinemia with relatively modest aminotransferase elevations, consistent with a predominantly cholestatic pattern of injury. Causality assessment using the Roussel Uclaf Causality Assessment Method (RUCAM) yielded a score of 7, indicating a probable association with deferasirox. Despite drug withdrawal and supportive care, including plasma exchange, liver failure persisted, and the patient was evaluated for urgent transplantation. This case underscores the vulnerability of patients with limited hepatic reserve to chelator-associated hepatotoxicity and highlights the need for individualized chelation strategies and close biochemical monitoring.

### [A] Real-world hospitalization and readmission trajectory after Guillain-Barré syndrome in the United States.

- PMID: 42983686
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: BMC neurology
- 作者: Voth J, Gerstmeyer J, Meuth SG, Zu Hörste GM, Willison AG, Konen FF, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 22
- DOI: 10.1186/s12883-026-05499-9
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42983686/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Guillain-Barré syndrome (GBS) is an acute immune-mediated polyradiculoneuropathy associated with substantial morbidity, but contemporary data on post-discharge readmission remain limited. We characterized 90-day readmission timing, causes, associated factors, and procedure coding after GBS.
METHODS: We analyzed an unweighted cohort of adults with a principal diagnosis of GBS in the 2020 Nationwide Readmissions Database. The primary outcome was all-cause readmission within 90 days after discharge. Readmission causes were classified using principal readmission diagnoses mapped to Clinical Classifications Software Refined categories, and procedure coding was descriptively assessed using ICD-10 procedure codes and procedure-day variables. Logistic regression models were used to identify factors associated with readmission.
RESULTS: Among 2,587 adults eligible for 90-day follow-up, 364 (14.1%) were readmitted. Readmissions clustered early, with 170 (46.7%) occurring within 15 days and 241 (66.2%) within 30 days. Separately, GBS was the principal diagnosis in 170 of 364 readmissions (46.7%), representing the largest grouped cause followed by septicemia (9.6%) and cardiovascular or thromboembolic conditions (6.9%). In adjusted analyses, discharge disposition was the most consistent marker of readmission risk: compared with routine home discharge, facility discharge was associated with higher odds of 90-day readmission (odds ratio 1.70, 95% confidence interval 1.27-2.28). Procedure-code timing suggested that diagnostic evaluation and immunotherapy were administered early, whereas plasma exchange, tracheostomy, gastrostomy, and rehabilitation-related procedures were performed at later stages.
CONCLUSIONS: Readmission after GBS is frequent, early, and clinically heterogeneous, supporting its interpretation as a marker of residual neurological disability, systemic complications, and care-transition vulnerability rather than recurrent GBS alone.

### [A] Therapeutic apheresis: An effective strategy for a combined targeting of circulating lipoproteins, inflammatory markers, PFAS, and microplastics in cardiometabolic and neurodegenerative disease?

- PMID: 42985345
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Brain health : lifelong brain resilience and longevity
- 作者: Bornstein SR, Kanczkowski W, Walther R, Kronstein-Wiedemann R, Fischer D, Oikonomakos I, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Aug 04
- DOI: 10.61373/bh026a.0024
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42985345/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

Lipoprotein apheresis is a highly effective and well-established extracorporeal therapy designed to remove lipoproteins from human circulation on a large scale, particularly in patients with progressive cardiovascular diseases when conventional treatments fail to achieve adequate lipoprotein reduction. Beyond its lipid-lowering capabilities, therapeutic apheresis is used to eliminate pathogenic substances, such as autoantibodies and immune complexes, and to exert immunomodulatory effects. Recent research highlights its potential to reduce inflammatory mediators, making it a promising intervention for conditions associated with systemic inflammation, including neurodegenerative diseases and post-infectious syndromes. In addition to its established applications, therapeutic apheresis is being explored as a novel approach to address emerging environmental health challenges, such as the accumulation of per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and micro- and nanoplastics (MNPs) in living organisms. These pollutants, which are increasingly pervasive in the environment, pose significant health risks, including metabolic disruption, carcinogenesis, and neuroinflammation. Recognizing the urgency of this issue, institutions such as the US Department of Health and Human Services have initiated research programs to develop methods to monitor and remove these contaminants from the human body. Here, our preliminary findings suggest that therapeutic apheresis may represent a promising approach for reducing environmental toxins, alongside its established role in lowering lipoproteins and inflammatory biomarkers, and warrants further systematic clinical investigation. This is based on the hypothesis that PFAS and MNPs form aggregates with lipids and proteins, suggesting that therapeutic apheresis could be an ideal method for clearance. In the initial results, we observed decreases in both PFAS and MNP levels in patient blood and sequestered plasma following double filtration plasmapheresis, although responses varied across individuals and analytes. Tissue and in vitro studies using imaging and functional bioassays may be suitable for demonstrating the potential of apheresis to remove PFAS and MNPs, as well as for assessing the impact of MNPs on cell viability, inflammation, and secretome profiles. These findings could serve as clinical surrogates to guide treatment frequency and evaluate the sustainability of therapeutic apheresis in mitigating health risks associated with environmental pollutants. While larger, well-designed clinical trials are needed to confirm these benefits, these early results could lay the groundwork for future research and clinical applications.

### [A] Therapeutic plasma exchange improves survival in alcohol-related acute-on-chronic liver failure: a randomized controlled trial.

- PMID: 42992479
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: European journal of gastroenterology & hepatology
- 作者: Sareen M, Sharma PN, Maharshi S, Sharma KK, Tak V, Nijhawan S, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 01
- DOI: 10.1097/MEG.0000000000003287
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42992479/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-08

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVES: Acute-on-chronic liver failure (ACLF) is associated with high short-term mortality and limited therapeutic options. Therapeutic plasma exchange (PLEX) may improve outcomes by removing inflammatory mediators, but randomized evidence in ACLF is limited. This study evaluated the efficacy of PLEX in alcohol-related ACLF (A-ACLF).
METHODS: In this randomized controlled trial, 100 patients with A-ACLF were randomized (1 : 1) to PLEX plus standard medical therapy (SMT) or SMT alone. PLEX was performed for up to three sessions. Coprimary outcomes were in-hospital mortality and 30-day survival. Secondary outcomes included hospital stay, ventilator requirement, and longitudinal changes in disease severity scores and laboratory parameters.
RESULTS: All 100 patients (PLEX n = 50; SMT n = 50) completed follow-up. In-hospital mortality was lower with PLEX [24 vs. 52%; P = 0.007], with an absolute risk reduction of 28% (number needed to treat: 3.6). Thirty-day survival was higher with PLEX (72 vs. 36%; P < 0.001), with an adjusted hazard ratio for mortality of 0.43 (P = 0.003). Significant improvements were observed in the model for end-stage liver disease sodium, Asian Pacific Association for the Study of the Liver ACLF Research Consortium, and Child-Turcotte-Pugh scores, and in international normalized ratio, creatinine, lactate, ammonia, and von Willebrand factor (all P < 0.001 for interaction). Hospital stay was shorter with PLEX (19 vs. 24 days; P = 0.023). No major PLEX-related adverse events were observed.
CONCLUSION: PLEX improves short-term survival and disease severity in A-ACLF and may be a valuable therapeutic option where transplantation is limited.

### [A] Rapidly progressive cognitive decline and drug-induced encephalopathy secondary to teprotumumab for thyroid eye disease: a case report and narrative literature review.

- PMID: 42991816
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in immunology
- 作者: Cheng ZJ, Zhou SX, He ZY, Deng ZF, Zeng C, Chen WP, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1985279
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42991816/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-08

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Teprotumumab, a fully human monoclonal antibody targeting insulin-like growth factor 1 receptor (IGF-1R), is an approved targeted therapy for thyroid eye disease (TED). Teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy is an extremely rare but potentially severe central nervous system adverse event, with only three cases reported globally to date. We report the first Han-Chinese patient with Graves' disease and associated TED complicated by teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy, together with a narrative review of all published cases, to raise clinicians' awareness and improve early recognition and management of this rare complication.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 56-year-old Chinese man with Graves' disease and TED developed acute progressive apathy and encephalopathy characterised by multidomain cognitive and behavioral dysfunction 20 days after the third teprotumumab infusion, with an admission MMSE score of 15. Contrast-enhanced brain MRI, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) examination, and autoimmune encephalitis antibody screening were unremarkable. Prolonged video-electroencephalography(VEEG) demonstrated intermittent diffuse bilateral slow-wave activity, and widespread interictal epileptiform discharges without electrographic or clinical seizures. Systematic workup excluded infectious, autoimmune, metabolic, and psychiatric encephalopathy. Treatment-emergent hyperglycemia was confirmed as a secondary aggravating factor rather than the primary cause. The patient achieved prominent neurological recovery after individualized treapeutic plasma exchange (TPE) and symptomatic management, with a Naranjo score of 6 indicating probable drug-related encephalopathy.
NARRATIVE LITERATURE REVIEW: Synthesis of all three previously published teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy cases demonstrates that neurological toxicity can develop as early as the third infusion cycle. These cases present consistent neuroimaging features with variable CSF profiles, and thyroid autoantibody elevation predominantly reflects pre-existing Graves' disease-related autoimmune background instead of directly mediating neurotoxicity. TPE generally facilitates neurological recovery but may be complicated by procedural hypersensitivity and fluctuating therapeutic responses. Notably, interictal epileptiform discharges on VEEG represent a newly identified neurophysiological manifestation of this rare adverse event. A three-hit interacting pathogenic hypothesis is proposed: impaired IGF-1R-dependent neurotrophic signalling serves as the core injury mechanism, while underlying thyroid autoimmunity and treatment-related metabolic disturbance act as susceptibility-modifying factors.
DISCUSSION: This case expands the ethnic and clinical spectrum of teprotumumab-related encephalopathy. Diffuse interictal epileptiform discharges without ictal changes reflect reactive cortical hyperexcitability secondary to acute encephalopathy. Metabolic disturbance merely exacerbated neurological impairment. Limitations include single-case constraints, simultaneous multi-intervention therapy, and lack of direct intracranial mechanistic evidence.
CONCLUSION: Teprotumumab-associated encephalopathy is a rare but serious adverse event. Close neurological surveillance and are essential during medication. Accumulated cases are needed to establish unified diagnostic and therapeutic criteria.

### [A] Thrombotic Complications in Patients With Coronavirus Disease 2019 (COVID-19): Two Contrasting Clinical Presentations Viewed Through the Concept of Thromboinflammation.

- PMID: 42990839
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Cureus
- 作者: Colakoglu C
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.117525
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42990839/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-08

#### 摘要

Thrombotic complications are well-recognized extrapulmonary manifestations of coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19). Although initially attributed to virus-specific endothelial injury, increasing evidence suggests that these events are better explained by thromboinflammatory mechanisms involving immunothrombosis. We present two contrasting thrombotic complications observed during acute severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) infection and discuss them within the framework of thromboinflammation and host susceptibility. The first patient was a previously healthy 35-year-old postpartum woman who developed acquired thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (TTP) characterized by microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, severe thrombocytopenia, acute kidney injury, neurological deterioration, reduced ADAMTS13 activity, and a favorable response to therapeutic plasma exchange. Despite hematological recovery, distal ischemic injury resulted in digital amputation. The second patient was a 61-year-old man with a heavy smoking history and a radiologically suspicious pulmonary malignancy who developed acute right femoral artery thrombosis during severe COVID-19 pneumonia, requiring emergency thromboembolectomy. Although revascularization was successful, distal tissue necrosis developed, whereas ischemic manifestations in the remaining extremities resolved with combined medical therapy. Both cases demonstrated distinct thrombotic phenotypes despite a common inflammatory trigger. These contrasting cases support the concept that severe systemic inflammation may produce different thrombotic manifestations according to underlying host susceptibility rather than reflecting a unique SARS-CoV-2-specific vasculopathy. The COVID-19 pandemic provided an unprecedented opportunity to observe diverse thromboinflammatory phenotypes, contributing to a broader understanding of thromboinflammation beyond COVID-19 itself.

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Catastrophic antiphospholipid syndrome (CAPS) is a rare, life-threatening variant of antiphospholipid syndrome marked by rapid multiorgan thrombosis. Early diagnosis remains challenging, especially in young adults.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 22-year-old woman with immune thrombocytopenia, Raynaud's phenomenon, and a recent cholecystectomy presented with headache, abdominal pain, and systemic symptoms. She developed thrombotic events in the brain, spleen, and kidneys. Laboratory testing demonstrated positive antiphospholipid antibodies, and renal biopsy confirmed thrombotic microangiopathy.
DISCUSSION: CAPS was diagnosed in the context of evolving systemic lupus erythematosus. Treatment included corticosteroids, plasma exchange, anticoagulation, hydroxychloroquine, and rituximab. Despite delayed recognition due to overlapping autoimmune conditions, multidisciplinary management led to clinical stabilization.
CONCLUSIONS: This case highlights the diagnostic complexity of CAPS in young patients with autoimmune comorbidities. Early recognition and aggressive multidisciplinary treatment are essential for favorable outcomes.

- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78432-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42971998/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-04

#### 摘要

Pulmonary hypertension, defined by elevated pressure in the pulmonary circulation, is morbid and diagnosing is challenging as no early-stage blood markers are currently available. Here we show that large-scale proteomic profiling across several independent observational cohorts identifies 22 proteins associated with echocardiography-derived pulmonary artery systolic pressure, several of which are independently associated with invasive measures of pulmonary vascular disease, structural remodeling of the pulmonary vasculature, and subsequent risk of pulmonary hypertension. Genetic analyses support a potential causal role for NOTCH3 and identify a common splice-regulating genetic variant that may modify susceptibility to pulmonary vascular stressors. Protein-based clustering further reveals two distinct biological subtypes associated with increased risk of pulmonary hypertension. Together, these findings identify candidate biomarkers of pulmonary hypertension, implicate pulmonary vascular remodeling in early-stage disease with a potential causal role for NOTCH3, and discover and validate two distinct endotypes of pulmonary hypertension risk in the community.

### [A] Venousization enables angiogenesis in the absence of VEGFR signalling.

- PMID: 42983084
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 跨领域顶刊启发
- 来源类型: 顶刊扩展
- 来源说明: Cell/Nature/Science生命科学顶刊
- 期刊: Nature
- 作者: Garcia-Gonzalez I, Rocha SF, Oliveira FV, Sanchez-Muñoz MS, Lytvyn M, Garcia-Cabero A, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 04
- DOI: 10.1038/s41586-026-11224-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42983084/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

The vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF) ligand and its main receptor VEGFR2 are considered to be essential for endothelial differentiation, proliferation, sprouting and survival1. Blood vessels cannot form in growing embryos or tissues when VEGF-VEGFR2 signalling is compromised in all cells2,3. The anti-VEGF antibody, which can block angiogenesis in cancer, wound healing or ischaemic diseases, is among the most widely used antibodies in the clinic. However, vascular resistance to anti-VEGF therapy has been reported4,5. Here we used iFlpMosaics and iSuRe-HadCre to induce and track genetic mosaics of endothelial cells (ECs) lacking VEGFR2 during the entire embryonic and postnatal development. These Vegfr2-knockout (Vegfr2KO) ECs adapted, proliferated normally and competed with wild-type cells over time, eventually forming a substantial portion of the vascular network in most organs. Vegfr2KO ECs could not sprout during the first wave of angiogenesis in most tissues, because this process is highly VEGF-dependent and favours growth and mobilization of wild-type ECs. However, owing to their inability to respond to VEGF, Vegfr2KO ECs became dominant in veins, which represented a long-term and continuous source of ECs for subsequent waves of VEGF-independent angiogenesis. Comparative single-cell RNA sequencing analysis of ECs with acute and long-term loss of VEGFR2 identified both common and organ-specific molecular mechanisms of adaptation and resistance to VEGF signalling loss. These included significant endothelial venousization and upregulation of ligands for VEGFR1 and VEGFR3. VEGFR1 only partially compensated for VEGFR2 loss in capillary ECs, whereas VEGFR3 compensated only in arterial ECs. Loss of the three VEGF receptors did not compromise venous growth. Notably, loss of the master regulator of venous development, NR2F2 (also known as COUP-TFII), significantly compromised expansion of Vegfr2KO ECs. This work has implications for our understanding of the role of VEGF receptors in angiogenesis and demonstrates venousization as a mechanism of adaptation and resistance to VEGF signalling loss during angiogenesis.

### [C] Anti-inflammation lipid nanoparticles enable localized mRNA therapy for inflammatory bowel disease.

- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78302-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42982860/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-06

#### 摘要

Inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) is an incurable disorder that requires long-term management, which is limited by suboptimal efficacy and systemic toxicity of current treatments. We developed a series of glucocorticoid-derived ionizable lipids and formulated them into lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) for rectal and lesion-localized mRNA delivery. MP2 LNPs, the lead formulation based on a methylprednisolone-derived ionizable lipid, showed a 15-fold higher delivery efficiency in vivo than the FDA approved SM102 LNPs. When loaded with interleukin-35 (IL-35) mRNA, MP2 LNPs (MP2-IL-35) ameliorated colitis in acute and chronic IBD mouse models, as shown by improved clinical scores, preserved colon length, and reduced pro-inflammatory cytokines. In ex vivo human colonic biopsies, MP2 LNPs enabled effective mRNA delivery in immune, epithelial and endothelial cells. Moreover, in biopsies from patients with IBD, MP2-IL-35 LNPs significantly reshaped effector and regulatory T cell subsets. Together, these findings support MP2-IL-35 LNPs as a promising mRNA therapy for IBD.

### [C] Dose-Dependent Autophagic Dysfunction and CCR5-Mediated Neuroinflammation Drive Arsenic Trioxide-Induced Cortical Neurodegeneration in Mice.

- PMID: 42936917
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of biochemical and molecular toxicology
- 作者: Koyya P, Sun Z, Manthari RK
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1002/jbt.71150
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42936917/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-02

#### 摘要

Neurotoxic environmental contaminant arsenic is widely present in global groundwater used for consumption and dietary sources, and has been significantly implicated in cognitive and neurodegenerative diseases. Although of increasing concern, the dose-dependent molecular pathways mediating arsenic-induced corticocerebral neurons dysfunction remain poorly understood, especially regarding the cross-talk between regulation of autophagy and neuroinflammation. This research explored the molecular mechanisms underlying the neurotoxic effects of arsenic trioxide (As2O3) in the mice cerebral cortex, particularly PI3K-AKT-mTOR-regulated autophagic activity and CCR5-induced microglia-driven neuroinflammation, at physiologically and experimentally relevant dosage. Thirty-two adult male Kunming mice were randomly assigned to four groups (n = 8) and orally administered 0, 0.15, 1.5, or 15 ppm As2O3 for 90 days. Quantitative real-time PCR, Western blot, hematoxylin and eosin histomorphometry and transmission electron microscopy were performed on cerebral cortex region. The PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway and autophagy-related genes ATG5, ATG7 and LC3 were significantly increased at low-level exposure reflecting adaptive response, but strongly down-regulated at lethal levels, with concurrent activation of mTOR, revealing defective autophagy. High level treatment led to significant up-regulation of GLS1, CCR5, CCL2, CCL3, CCL5 and Iba1, reflecting glutaminolytic switch and threshold-dependent microglial neuroinflammation. Histological morphometry supported dose-dependent neurodegeneration, with healthy neurons decreasing and the pyknotic index increasing at high doses. Electron microscopy confirmed cytoplasmic vacuole accumulation, mitochondrial cristae distortion, and autophagosomes (AP)-lysosomes (LY) co-accumulation, indicating compromised autophagic flux. These findings reveal that high arsenic exposure may be associated with neurodegeneration involving convergent mTOR-driven autophagic dysfunction, an excitotoxic metabolic shift and CCR5-associated neuroinflammation, pointing to potential mechanistically interlinked therapeutic targets that warrant further functional validation.

### [C] Mechanistic insights of Artemisia-derived therapeutics in Alzheimer's disease (AD): from crude botanical extracts to artemisinin and derivatives.

- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1007/s13205-026-05110-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42935657/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-02

#### 摘要

Alzheimer's disease (AD) is characterized by several pathological processes including amyloid-β (Aβ) accumulation, Tau hyperphosphorylation, oxidative stress, synaptic dysfunction, neuroinflammation, mitochondrial impairment and loss in neuronal stability. This review assessed the reported neuroprotective potential of crude extracts of different Artemisia species and artemisinin with its derivatives in AD. It focuses on targets, mechanisms, observational markers and functional effects validated by Artemisia extracts and compounds like artemisinin, artemisinin B, artemether, artesunate and dihydroartemisinin in experimental AD models. A comprehensive review of studies was conducted including in vitro, in vivo and in silico research on Artemisia extracts and derivatives focusing on their effects on various pathological processes related to brain. Results showed crude extracts from 17 Artemisia species, which have been reported with considerable neuroprotective effects. Artemisinin and derivatives deliver neuroprotection via targeted mechanisms and effecting observational markers with varying functional outcomes. However, differences in AD models, administration routes, doses, extract composition and outcomes limit direct efficacy comparisons. Hence, direct comparisons of efficacy among Artemisia extracts, artemisinin and its derivatives should be interpreted cautiously. Future inquiries should highlight standardized preparations, brain exposure and pharmacokinetics, mechanistic validation, long-term safety and well-designed comparative and clinical trials to evaluate their potential for AD treatment.

### [C] Articular Cartilage-Derived Extracellular Matrix Material Promotes a Pro-Regenerative Macrophage Phenotype to Support Chondrogenesis.

- PMID: 42910446
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
